## Plan: Simplify PackageKind/classify_package System

### Summary

Replace the 5-variant `PackageKind` enum and directory-based `classify_package()` function with a simple `is_build_tool: bool` field on `PackageMember`. Remove `test_members()` and the special test-binary-discovery code in `test_all()`. The only behavioral distinction that matters is "exclude build tools from batch operations."

### File-by-File Changes

#### 1. `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/workspace.rs` (primary changes)

**Remove:**
- The entire `PackageKind` enum (lines 11-23)
- The `kind: PackageKind` field from `PackageMember` (line 31)
- The entire `classify_package()` function (lines 116-157)
- The `test_members()` method (lines 107-112)

**Add:**
- `is_build_tool: bool` field to `PackageMember`
- A simple `fn is_build_tool(name: &str) -> bool` helper that checks `name == "tspec" || name == "xt" || name == "xtask"`

**Modify `WorkspaceInfo::discover()`:**
- Replace the `classify_package` call and POP special-casing with:
  ```rust
  let is_build_tool = !is_pop && is_build_tool_name(&pkg.name);
  ```
- Remove the `kind` field from `PackageMember` construction, add `is_build_tool`

**Modify `buildable_members()`:**
- Change filter from `m.kind != PackageKind::BuildTool` to `!m.is_build_tool`

**Modify `runnable_members()`:**
- Change filter from `m.has_binary && m.kind == PackageKind::App` to `m.has_binary && !m.is_build_tool`
- This is a behavioral change: previously `Tool` and `Lib` with binaries were excluded from `runnable_members()`. Now any non-build-tool with a binary is runnable. This is correct -- tspec should not impose layout opinions.

**Replace unit tests:**
Delete all `classify_*` tests (classify_app, classify_root_package_is_app, classify_lib, classify_root_level_member_lib_no_binary, classify_root_level_member_app_with_binary, classify_tool, classify_test_by_path, classify_test_by_name, classify_build_tool_xt, classify_build_tool_xtask, classify_lib_under_tests_dir_not_misclassified).

Replace with:
- `is_build_tool_recognizes_known_names` -- test that "tspec", "xt", "xtask" return true
- `is_build_tool_rejects_regular_names` -- test that "myapp", "tspec-build" return false
- Keep `discover_works` but update it: remove `PackageKind` references, check `is_build_tool` instead. Verify tspec itself is excluded from `buildable_members()` (it is a build tool). Verify `runnable_members()` returns only members with `has_binary && !is_build_tool`.

#### 2. `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/all.rs` (simplify test_all)

**Remove:**
- `use crate::workspace::PackageKind` from imports (line 17) -- only `WorkspaceInfo` needed
- The `PackageKind::Test` skip in `test_all()` (lines 270-272)
- The entire "Handle test packages" block in `test_all()` (lines 319-428) -- the test-binary-discovery logic
- The `use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;` import (line 5) -- only needed for the test binary permissions check

**Result:** `test_all()` becomes straightforward -- iterate `buildable_members()`, call `test_package()` on each. Same structure as `build_all()` but calling `test_package` instead of `build_package`.

The `use crate::run::run_binary` import can also be removed since it was only used in the test-binary-discovery block.

#### 3. `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/cmd/test.rs` (no changes needed)

This file calls `workspace.buildable_members()` at line 117 which will continue to work unchanged -- the method signature and return type are the same.

#### 4. No other files need changes

The grep confirms no other files reference `PackageKind`, `test_members`, or `classify_package`. The `build.rs`, `run.rs`, and `compare.rs` commands only call `WorkspaceInfo::discover()` and the filter methods, which keep their signatures.

### Behavioral Changes

1. **`runnable_members()` is more permissive**: Previously only `PackageKind::App` with binaries. Now any non-build-tool with a binary. This means a package in `tools/` or at workspace root level with a binary would now be runnable in `run_all()`. This is the correct behavior -- no layout opinions.

2. **`test_all()` no longer has special test-binary-discovery**: Packages formerly classified as `Test` (name ending in `-tests`, path containing `/tests`) will now be tested via normal `cargo test -p <name>` like everything else. If the workspace doesn't contain such packages (and this repo doesn't), there is zero behavioral difference.

3. **POPs no longer get special `PackageKind::App` assignment**: They just get `is_build_tool: false`, which is correct since a POP is never a build tool.

### Sequencing

This is a single-commit refactor. All changes are tightly coupled -- you cannot remove `PackageKind` without also updating the filter methods and `test_all()`. The steps within the commit:

1. Add `is_build_tool_name()` helper function
2. Replace `kind: PackageKind` with `is_build_tool: bool` on `PackageMember`
3. Update `discover()` to set `is_build_tool` instead of `kind`
4. Update `buildable_members()` and `runnable_members()` filters
5. Remove `test_members()`
6. Remove `classify_package()` and `PackageKind` enum
7. Simplify `test_all()` in `all.rs` (remove test-binary-discovery block, remove `PackageKind` import)
8. Replace unit tests in `workspace.rs`
9. Run verification: `tspec test -p tspec && tspec test -p tspec-build && tspec clippy && tspec fmt --check`

### Potential Issues

- **Compilation order**: Since `PackageKind` is `pub`, removing it is a breaking change to the public API. But `workspace.rs` types are only used internally (not re-exported from `lib.rs` or used by `tspec-build`), so this is fine.
- **The `PermissionsExt` import removal**: Verify no other code in `all.rs` uses it (the grep shows it is only used in the test-binary block).

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/workspace.rs` - Primary target: remove PackageKind enum, classify_package(), add is_build_tool field, rewrite tests
- `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/all.rs` - Remove test-binary-discovery block from test_all(), remove PackageKind import
- `/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/cmd/test.rs` - Verify no changes needed (uses buildable_members() which keeps its signature)
